#!name gen0

set -- -z -- -z; unset OPTIND; OPTIND=1; while getopts :ab o 2>&1; do echo "$o|${OPTARG-U}|$OPTIND"; done; echo "end $OPTIND"
alias a='unalias a; echo A'; a; a 2>&1; echo rc=$?
